# LDO-QUIET-SKY130

Design a continuous-time analog LDO meeting spec.json across conditions.json and the specified load range.
Create dut.spice with `.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF` and `.ends DUT`.
Choose connectivity, bias, compensation and dimensions yourself. No starting circuit is supplied.
Use only the declared MOS family and positive R/C in devices.md. Read measurement.md.
You have 90 minutes of cumulative model-call time with no native-case limit. Use the client to measure and submit your fixed circuit before the deadline.
No Web search. Use only the provided task information and your own knowledge.
Measurements consume cases even when simulation fails. Submit through the client with a rationale, then end your session.

## Time accounting

The budget counts model calls, including provider queuing and transport. ngspice, Python, filesystem tools and their waits do not consume this budget. Model calls overlapping local tools still count. An already admitted measurement may finish after model-budget exhaustion; new design tools close. Use client status for the latest collected model-time reading.

# LDO-QUIET-SKY130 measurement contract

Numerical stimuli and limits come from spec.json. Connect the same submitted
DUT to VIN, VOUT, VSS and ideal noiseless VREF. The load is a prescribed ideal
finite-slew current sink; the external capacitor is ideal and bench supplied.
No startup, protection, bandgap, yield or layout checks are implied.

## DC and load changes

Run DC at all three loads and light/heavy, middle/heavy and burst profiles.
Meter all DUT terminals. Input/reference currents enter DUT; output/ground
currents leave. Verify KCL within 1 nA. Overhead is
max(Iin-Iout,0)+max(Iref,0), including feedback and bias consumption.
Compare voltage against the fixed 1.2 V target, never the waveform tail.
Both isolated edge directions and the final falling burst edge have recovery
windows. Recovery is the final interpolated entry into the absolute required
band that remains there until the window ends. Retain overshoot, droop, initial
hold error, profile points and all signed currents.

Maximum sample gap is 1e-08 s. Every required window must be fully
covered with finite increasing data. Unsettled complete data retains null
recovery and an observation lower bound; it is MISS when that bound exceeds the
limit. Missing/truncated/invalid data is measurement failure. Unexecuted cases
are incomplete, never PASS. Inclusive limits are compared without rounding.

## Supply measurements

Line stimulus starts at minimum VIN, rises to maximum, and returns to minimum,
at the group's highest load, process, temperature and capacitance. The starting
VIN label does not add a different line trajectory. PSRR saves native VIN/VOUT
phasors at 1 kHz and 1 MHz and reports 20 log10(abs(VIN/VOUT)). Check the native
regulated OP; zero/nonfinite transfers cannot produce infinite rejection.

PSRR, bidirectional line excursion/recovery and output noise are scored in every
public and independent group. Each nominal group contains 11 native processes:
three DC, three load profiles, two PSRR, two noise and one line profile.
There are 198 public plus 33 independent cases, with no duplicate nominal
characterization stage. Standalone noise_low/noise_high/line/PSRR cost one each.
Line uses the tighter line excursion limit; its initial and recovery bands are
the fixed DC/recovery bands. Repeated endpoint trajectories across starting-VIN
labels are repetitions, not extra coverage.

Output noise integrates native output voltage ASD squared from 10 Hz to 100 kHz
at 50 points/decade. Use a linear-frequency trapezoidal PSD integral and then its
square root. VREF and load are ideal noiseless sources. Keep the spectrum in
V/sqrt(Hz), integrated output in V RMS, and a valid regulated OP. Negative ASD,
missing band endpoints, sparse/nonfinite data and nonregulating OP cannot pass.

## Numerical precision

Independent evaluation uses the captured standard grid for every required case.
Numerical precision comparisons use a separately authorized developer allocation.
A standard-grid PASS is not a per-submission convergence proof.
# Shared client

Write request.json and call `atb-client --request request.json`.
Commands: `atb-client help`, `atb-client status`, `atb-client validate`,
`atb-client estimate --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client measure --id m001 --analysis nominal --pvt N`,
`atb-client submit --rationale "Design rationale"`.
Use `--cl` in farads and optional `--seed` only when the task supports it.
The client reads dut.spice once for validate/measure/submit and sends its exact UTF-8 contents.
Measure requires experiment_id, analysis and pvt; cl is optional. Use help for analyses and native case costs.
Use a new experiment_id when changing a circuit or measurement request. Completed identical requests reuse their saved result.
Submit requires rationale. Successful submission freezes that circuit for every independent condition and closes exploration.

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

# Netlist grammar

Use local .subckt/.ends, local .param and constant arithmetic + - * / in braces.
Elements are X for approved MOS wrappers or local subcircuits, R and C. No includes, models, sources or analyses.
MOS order is drain gate source body. NFET body connects to VSS; PFET body connects to VIN.
Use VSS instead of global node 0. Names are case-insensitive. Full-line * comments and + continuation are supported.
SKY130 W/L are bare numeric micrometres; nf=1 and integer m=1..32.
ADM45 W/L use SI or SPICE suffixes; integer mult=1..32, implicit nf=1, default ad/as/pd/ps=0.
R/C use SI or suffixes (M is milli, meg is mega). Positive R=1..1e8 ohm, C>=1e-15 F; total C and MOS W*L must satisfy spec.json.
Maximum source 65536 bytes, 2048 statements, 32 local subcircuits, depth 12, 2048 expansion visits, 512 expanded elements.
Only used subcircuits are allowed. Validation checks each physical copy and each technology's geometry domain.

Per-polarity domains (micrometres), intersect all tt/ss/ff sections and W<=100, L>=0.15:

```json
```
MODEL

I’m starting with a PMOS pass device and a mirrored error amplifier so the control node has enough voltage range at both supply extremes. The first measurements will check regulation, bias current, and stability before I tune noise and supply rejection.

ACTION
0:03:14
Bash
/bin/bash -lc "cat > dut.spice <<'EOF'
* PMOS LDO with a current-mirror error amplifier
.subckt DUT VIN VOUT VSS VREF
RFB1 VOUT FB 30k
RFB2 FB VSS 30k
* Supply-referenced bias
RBIAS BP VSS 200k

/bin/bash -lc "cp dut.spice v001.spice
cat > summarize.py <<'PY'
import json,sys
for fn in sys.argv[1:]:
 d=json.load(open(fn)); print(fn, d.get('ok'), d.get('revision'),d.get('budget',{}).get('remaining_seconds'))
 if not d.get('ok'): print(d);continue
 for c in d.get('cases',[]):
  m=c.get('measurements',{}); s={k:m[k] for k in ['vout_v','overhead_a','psrr_db'] if k in m}
  if 'noise' in m:s['noise']=m['noise'].get('output_noise_rms_v')
  for k in ['initial_hold','envelope']:
   if k in m:s[k]={a:m[k][a] for a in ['min_v','max_v']}

import json,sys,subprocess
from pathlib import Path
ident,analysis,pvt=sys.argv[1:4]
req={'action':'measure','experiment_id':ident,'analysis':analysis,'pvt':pvt}
if len(sys.argv)>4:req['cl']=float(sys.argv[4])
Path(ident+'.request.json').write_text(json.dumps(req))
Path(ident+'.spice').write_text(Path('dut.spice').read_text())
with open(ident+'.json','w') as f:subprocess.run(['atb-client','--request',ident+'.request.json'],stdout=f,check=True)
subprocess.run(['python3','summarize.py',ident+'.json'])
PY
python3 - <<'PY'
from pathlib import Path
s=Path('dut.spice').read_text()
for name,drain,gate in [('XNR','NR','NR'),('XNF','NF','NF'),('XNR2','NP','NR'),('XNO','GP','NF')]:
 old=next(x for x in s.splitlines() if x.startswith(name+' '))
 new=old.replace(name+' '+drain+' ',name+' '+name+'D ',1)
 new+='\\n'+name+'C '+drain+' BN '+name+'D VSS sky130_fd_pr__nfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=4'
 s=s.replace(old,new)
for name,drain in [('XNP','NP'),('XPO','GP')]:
 old=next(x for x in s.splitlines() if x.startswith(name+' '))
 new=old.replace(name+' '+drain+' ',name+' '+name+'D ',1).replace('w=100 l=1 nf=1 m=16','w=50 l=1 nf=1 m=16')
 new+='\\n'+name+'C '+drain+' BP '+name+'D VIN sky130_fd_pr__pfet_01v8 w=100 l=0.5 nf=1 m=8'

s=s.replace('.ends DUT','CFEED GP VIN 2p\\n.ends DUT')

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess,sys,json
prefix=sys.argv[1]
base=Path('dut.spice').read_text()
Path(prefix+'.spice').write_text(base)
for cl in [1e-7,1e-6]:
 for pvt in ['SLH','FLC','FLH','FHC','SHC','SHH','N','SLC','FHH']:
  if cl==1e-7 and pvt in ['N','SLC','FHH'] and '--skip-three' in sys.argv:continue
  if Path('dut.spice').read_text()"'!=base:raise RuntimeError('"'Circuit changed during grid')
  ident=f'{prefix}_{pvt}_{int(cl*1e9)}'
  with open(ident+'.console.log','w') as log:subprocess.run(['python3','run.py',ident,'nominal',pvt,str(cl)],stdout=log,check=True)
  subprocess.run(['python3','compact.py',ident+'.json'],check=True)
  print('',flush=True)
PY"

/bin/bash -lc "cat > audit.py <<'PY'
import json,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
files=[Path(f'm{n:03d}.json') for n in [37,38,39]]+list(Path('.').glob('g01_*.json'))
rows=[];seen={};revs=set();hashes=set();resources=None
for p in files:
 d=json.load(p.open())
 if 'cases' not in d:continue
 revs.add(d['revision']);hashes.add(d['summary']['params']['source_sha256'])
 resources=d['summary']['params']['resources']
 for c in d['cases']:seen[(d['pvt'],d['cl'],c['case_id'])]=c['status']
 for r in d['summary']['spec_table']:rows.append(dict(r,pvt=d['pvt'],cl=d['cl'],file=p.name))
worst={}
for r in rows:
 k=r['metric'];v=r['measured']
 if v is None:continue
 if k not in worst or (v>worst[k]['measured'] if r['direction']=='<=' else v<worst[k]['measured']):worst[k]=r
fails=[r for r in rows if r['verdict']"'!='"'PASS']
case_ids=['dc_low','dc_mid','dc_high','light_heavy','mid_heavy','burst','psrr_low','psrr_high','noise_low','noise_high','line']
expected={(p,c,i) for p in json.load(open('conditions.json')) for c in [1e-7,1e-6] for i in case_ids}
missing=sorted(expected-set(seen))
sha=hashlib.sha256(Path('dut.spice').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
report={'status':'PASS' if not missing and not fails and all(v=='OK' for v in seen.values()) and len(revs)==1 and hashes=={sha} else 'INCOMPLETE_OR_FAIL','revision':sorted(revs),'source_sha256':sha,'source_matches':hashes=={sha},'native_cases_complete':len(seen),'native_cases_required':198,'checks':len(rows),'resources':resources,'failed_checks':fails,'missing_cases':missing,'worst':worst}
Path('public-validation.json').write_text(json.dumps(report,indent=2)+'\\n')
print(report['status'],len(seen),'/198 cases;',len(fails),'failed checks; source matches',report['source_matches'])
for k in ['dc_error_v','overhead_a','psrr_1000_db','psrr_1000000_db','output_noise_rms_v','recovery_0_s','recovery_1_s']:
 if k in worst:
  r=worst[k]; print(k,r['measured'],r['pvt'],r['cl'],r['case_id'])
for r in fails:print('FAIL',r['pvt'],r['cl'],r['case_id'],r['metric'],r['measured'],r.get('measured_lower_bound'))
print('Resources',resources)

/bin/bash -lc "python3 - <<'PY'
import json,hashlib
from pathlib import Path
r=json.load(open('public-validation.json'))
assert r['status']=='PASS' and r['native_cases_complete']==198
assert hashlib.sha256(Path('dut.spice').read_bytes()).hexdigest()==r['source_sha256']
w=r['worst']; e=r['extrema']
notes=f'''The fixed DUT is a continuous-time PMOS LDO with a cascoded PMOS input amplifier and an NMOS-controlled 24:1 PMOS pass mirror. A 14.975 kohm resistor matches the reference-input source resistance to the feedback divider, reducing conversion of supply transients into differential input error. VIN-derived MOS/resistor bias sets the amplifier current. An 80 pF capacitor in series with 130 kohm, together with a 10 pF shunt capacitor, compensates the loop.

The exact netlist passed all 198 public native cases and all 612 reported checks across nine PVT groups and both external-capacitor endpoints. These are standard-grid public results; independent evaluation occurs through submission.

| Quantity | Worst public measurement | Limit |
|---|---:|---:|
| DC error | {w['dc_error_v']['measured']*1e3:.3f} mV | 12 mV |
| Load excursion | {e['load_excursion_v']['measured']*1e3:.3f} mV | 48 mV |
| Line excursion | {e['line_excursion_v']['measured']*1e3:.3f} mV | 24 mV |
| Recovery | {e['recovery_s']['measured']*1e6:.3f} us | 20 us |
| Light-load overhead | {e['light_load_overhead_a']['measured']*1e6:.3f} uA | 100 uA |
| Overall overhead | {w['overhead_a']['measured']*1e6:.3f} uA | 300 uA |
| PSRR at 1 kHz | {w['psrr_1000_db']['measured']:.3f} dB | 40 dB minimum |
| PSRR at 1 MHz | {w['psrr_1000000_db']['measured']:.3f} dB | 30 dB minimum |
| Output noise, 10 Hz–100 kHz | {w['output_noise_rms_v']['measured']*1e6:.3f} uV RMS | 30 uV RMS |
| Explicit capacitance | 90 pF | 100 pF |
| MOS W*L area proxy | 6417 um2 | 10000 um2 |

Revision: "'`{r['"'revision'][0]}"'`. Source SHA-256: `{r['"'source_sha256']}"'`. Full numerical evidence is summarized in `public-validation.json`.
'"'''
Path('design-notes.md').write_text(notes)
rationale='Fixed 1.2 V continuous-time LDO using a cascoded PMOS input amplifier, matched reference/feedback source resistances, and an NMOS-controlled 24:1 PMOS pass mirror. VIN-derived bias provides the operating currents. An 80 pF/130 kohm series compensation branch and 10 pF shunt support the 100 nF to 1 uF output range. This exact DUT passed all 198 public native cases and 612 checks. Worst public noise is 20.7825 uV RMS, minimum PSRR is 42.9779 dB at 1 kHz and 33.9993 dB at 1 MHz, and maximum recovery is 14.6263 us. Explicit capacitance is 90 pF and MOS W*L area is 6417 um2.'
Path('submission-request.json').write_text(json.dumps({'action':'submit','rationale':rationale},indent=2)+'\\n')
print('Submission prepared for',r['revision'][0])
PY
atb-client --request submission-request.json > submission.json

import json
x=json.load(open('submission.json'))
print(json.dumps(x,indent=2)[:18000])
PY"
